Kết quả chấm bài

Substantiated behaviors: rw_lab.exe execution from cmd.exe (E02), file encryption to *.locked (E03), ransom note placement (E04), failed shadow deletion (E05), no persistence (E06), no network activity (E07). Unique defects: 1. Claimed-only C2 communication; 2. Claimed-only registry activity inference; 3. Missing hash in chunk_6; 4. Placeholder in registry section; 5. Unresolved evidence reference in chunk_11.

chunk_1

### Bảng 2

| Nội dung | Ghi nhận |
| --- | --- |
